Covers adding MIDI/OSC sources, feature indices (CC, Note Velocity, Beat Clock, Iterator Notes), Learn, controller mapping (MIDImix, Launchpad), OSCSender, playlist control by MIDI/OSC, and what Magic cannot do (no Ableton Link, NDI, DMX, MIDI out) with working bridges. Use when wiring controllers, DAWs, beat sync or external apps to Magic. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-midi-osc-control))
- **mmv-output-mapping**: Get Magic Music Visuals output to screens, projectors and other apps - Syphon/Spout server output, graphics resolution and aspect, fullscreen on a chosen display, feeding projection-mapping and media-server apps (Millumin, MadMapper, Resolume, VDMX, OBS), NDI bridging, edge blending and calibration patterns, movie export, and receiving other apps via SyphonClient. Use when planning outputs, mapping a surface, diagnosing resolution or sync issues, or building a calibration scene. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-output-mapping))
- **mmv-scene-recipes**: Ready scene recipes for Magic Music Visuals with generator scripts and shaders - retro sunset / synthwave (sun, perspective grid, mountains, stars, bloom, CRT; five palettes), calibration pattern, Music X-Ray (live audio analysis made visible: beat, drops, spectrum, tempo), audio spectrum, 3D cube field, kaleidoscope, glitch drop, feedback trails, lyrics overlay, Syphon compositing. Use when asked to build a specific look quickly, when starting the VJ Retro Sunset scene, or when you need a worked end-to-end example of Builder + ISF + audio Globals. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-scene-recipes))
- **mmv-scenes-and-connections**: Build and edit the structure of Magic Music Visuals scenes - modules, pin order, layering and compositing (Blend/Mask/Mix/MultiMix/InputSelector), scene modules, SceneInput effects, post-processing scenes, Iterator repeats, scene groups and tabs. Use when wiring modules together, deciding what draws in front, splitting a project into reusable scenes, or generating a scene graph with the Builder. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-scenes-and-connections))
- **mmv-shaders-isf-glsl**: Write, port, lint and debug shaders for Magic Music Visuals - ISF 2.0 generators and effects, audio FFT shaders, multi-pass and persistent-buffer feedback, GLSLShader (Shadertoy/GLSL Sandbox style) files, custom module folders, and the ISF AI Prompt window. Use when creating a new look in GLSL, porting a Shadertoy shader, fixing an "ISF Code Errors" dialog, or deciding how a custom .fs gets loaded into a generated project. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-shaders-isf-glsl))
- **mmv-vj-performance**: Run Magic Music Visuals live - playlist entries, transitions, auto-advance, scene switching by MIDI/OSC, Preview Mode for live editing, frame-rate budget and GPU/VRAM management, safe-load and crash-recovery habits, strobe safety, and a pre-show checklist. Use when preparing a gig, structuring a set across scenes, fixing dropped frames or audio lag, or setting up unattended startup. ([abossard/mmv-skills](https://github.com/abossard/mmv-skills/tree/HEAD/mmv-vj-performance))
- **mmvc-unity**: Implements and debugs Unity MMVC components using the SavableObservable framework (com.evialdev.savableobservable). This skill should be used for observable data models, Logic and Presenter classes, AutoBind and ObservableHandler bindings, dynamic component initialization, subscription cleanup, and custom save/load integration. It does not cover unrelated MVC or MVVM frameworks. ([alexxtaurus83/unity-savableobservable](https://github.com/alexxtaurus83/unity-savableobservable/tree/HEAD/mmvc-unity-skill) · ★1)
- **mmvp-eval**: Evaluates the visual reasoning and grounding capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on basic visual patterns such as orientation, counting, viewpoint, and feature presence. It specifically probes whether models fail due to limitations in their visual encoders (e.g., CLIP) rather than language model hallucinations. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMVP, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmvp-eval) · ★3)
- **mmw-paper-human-writing**: 中文数学建模论文写作与改稿 Skill。凡用户要求撰写、重写、润色、去 AI 味、降低模板感、 改善摘要或结果分析、检查数学建模论文中文表达时，都应使用本 Skill。它以证据推进和 学术可追溯性为核心，保护 LaTeX 公式、数值、单位、引用、图表编号和 MMW 方法追踪信息， 不把论文改成知乎长帖，也不靠禁冒号、禁破折号或随机句长伪装成人类写作。 ([liwenzhonhg-arch/Mathematical_Modeling_Workflow](https://github.com/liwenzhonhg-arch/Mathematical_Modeling_Workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/mmw-paper-human-writing) · ★3)
- **mmwp-eval**: Evaluates large language models' ability to perform mathematical, commonsense, and natural language inference reasoning in low-, medium-, and high-resource languages. It specifically probes cross-lingual transfer capabilities using a zero-shot chain-of-thought setting without requiring parallel multilingual instruction data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMWP, MGSM, MSVAMP, X-CSQA, XNLI, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmwp-eval) · ★3)

- **mneme-remember**: REMEMBER durable knowledge into mneme long-term memory: scan the session for facts, open questions, decisions, authored significance, and hard-won gotchas worth keeping across sessions, save each as a scoped user-profile, agent-continuity/practice, project or ordinary-work node in its explicitly selected owner, and wire grounded associations. Trigger on "remember this", "save to memory", "note this for later", "don't forget X", or a deliberate checkpoint before context is compacted. Builds on the `mneme` skill (how to drive it, selected owner scopes). ([nia-e/mneme](https://github.com/nia-e/mneme/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mneme-remember) · ★2)
- **mneme-review-closure**: Triage or close an external, adversarial, or re-review finding set against Mneme with one-to-one dispositions, implementation evidence, sibling-surface attacks, and a counter-report. Use for read-only finding triage or, with repository-write authority, for saving feedback and fixing findings. Saving artifacts or editing code requires repository-write authority; creating commits requires separate explicit commit authority. Never infer either from a read-only review request. ([nia-e/mneme](https://github.com/nia-e/mneme/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mneme-review-closure) · ★2)
- **mneme-search**: Use when the user asks a factual question whose answer might live in the vault. Invokes mneme_search. Production `mneme_search` is FTS5 BM25. ([OnourImpram/mneme](https://github.com/OnourImpram/mneme/tree/HEAD/packages/mneme-codex-plugin/skills/mneme-search) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **mneme-search**: Use when the user asks a factual question whose answer might live in the vault.
